# End-to-End Runtime Chain Map

Each row is backed by the structured `runtime_chain` object in `CAPABILITY_REGISTRY.json`; `intermediate_symbols` there records path-plus-symbol evidence. An empty stage means that stage does not apply or the capability has repository-wide not-present proof; it never authorises deletion by itself.

| Capability | Decision | Presence | User action | UI components | Routes | Hooks | Stores | Preload | Type contract | IPC | Handlers | Main/service symbols | Repositories | Data/native/process | Result events | Renderer update | Runtime registrations | Data read | Data written | Side effects | Failure | Cleanup | Recovery | Tests | Current breakage | Required change | Target owner |
